  46:     /**
  47:      * Replay warm-up is context-only. It gives the production analysis/execution services
  48:      * the same already-closed 1m/5m/1h history they would have had at the requested start
  49:      * without allowing wallet executions before the requested test window.
  50:      */
  51:     private static final Duration REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP = Duration.ofHours(3);
  52: 
  53:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  54:     // FIX-136: field injection preserves the existing constructor used by Replay tests.
  55:     @org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired

  66:     // FIX-087: heartbeat age alone cannot prove that a long replay worker has died.
  67:     // Some deterministic replay stages can legitimately run for more than the stale-heartbeat
  68:     // threshold without returning to updateRun(). Keep atomic in-JVM ownership so Resume cannot
  69:     // launch a second worker for the same durable run while the first worker is still executing.
  70:     private final Set<Long> activeRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  71: 
  72:     // FIX-090: cooperative stop requests are tracked separately from active ownership. A Stop Test
  73:     // request never pretends the worker is gone; Delete Data stays blocked until runAsync reaches
  74:     // finally and removes the run from activeRunIds.
  75:     private final Set<Long> stopRequestedRunIds = ConcurrentHashMap.newKeySet();
  76: 
  77:     public boolean isActive(long runId) {
  78:         return activeRunIds.contains(runId);
  79:     }
  80: 
  81:     public boolean hasActiveRuns() {
  82:         return !activeRunIds.isEmpty();
  83:     }
  84: 
  85:     public boolean requestStop(long runId) {
  86:         if (!activeRunIds.contains(runId)) return false;
  87:         stopRequestedRunIds.add(runId);
  88:         return true;
  89:     }
  90: 
  91:     public boolean isStopRequested(long runId) {
  92:         return stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId);
  93:     }
  94: 
  95:     private void checkStopRequested(long runId) {
  96:         if (stopRequestedRunIds.contains(runId)) throw new ReplayCancellationException(runId);
  97:     }
  98: 
  99:     @Async
 100:     public void runAsync(long runId, ReplayDataSource dataSource) {
 101:         // FIX-087: close the race at worker entry as well. Even if two callers schedule the same
 102:         // run almost simultaneously, only one may execute the deterministic replay pipeline.
 103:         if (!activeRunIds.add(runId)) {
 104:             log.warn("FIX-087: replay run {} already has an active worker; duplicate execution skipped", runId);
 105:             return;
 106:         }
 107:         // FIX-11J: Replay-only observational timers. The worker pipeline below is intentionally
 108:         // left in its existing order; these locals only capture elapsed monotonic time and are
 109:         // persisted once in finally so diagnostics cannot steer or fail Replay business logic.
 110:         final long replayTotalStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 111:         Long loadHistoricalDataNs = null;
 112:         Long verifyEventResolutionNs = null;
 113:         Long buildReplayDatasetNs = null;
 114:         Long generateFreshSignalsNs = null;
 115:         Long shadowExecutionNs = null;
 116:         Long parityComparisonNs = null;
 117:         try (var inputAudit = sharedMarketEnabled ? com.crypto.shared.CandleInputAudit.open(jdbcTemplate,auditTransactionManager,"REPLAY:"+runId) : null) {
 118:             long stageStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 119:             Map<String, Object> run = jdbcTemplate.queryForMap(
 120:                     "SELECT symbol, start_time, end_time FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id = ?", runId);
 121:             String symbol = String.valueOf(run.get("symbol"));
 122:             Instant start = toInstant(run.get("start_time"));
 123:             Instant end = toInstant(run.get("end_time"));
 124: 
 125:             updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", 2, "Loading historical candles and signals", null);
 126:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 127: 
 128:             Instant contextStart = start.minus(REPLAY_CONTEXT_WARMUP);
 129:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "1m", contextStart, end);
 130:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles = candles(symbol, "5m", contextStart, end);
 131:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles = candles(symbol, "1h", contextStart, end);
 132:             List<Candle> requestedOneMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneMinuteCandles, "1m", start, end);
 133:             List<Candle> requestedFiveMinuteCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(fiveMinuteCandles, "5m", start, end);
 134:             List<Candle> requestedOneHourCandles = candlesInExecutionWindow(oneHourCandles, "1h", start, end);
 135: 
 136:             String btcSymbol = btcContextProperties.referenceSymbol();
 137:             boolean replayBtcContext = btcContextProperties.enabled() && !btcSymbol.equalsIgnoreCase(symbol);
 138:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 139:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "5m", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 140:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles = replayBtcContext ? candles(btcSymbol, "1h", contextStart, end) : List.of();
 141: 
 142:             List<TradeSignal> sourceSignals = signalRepository
 143:                     .findBySymbolAndGeneratedAtBetweenOrderByGeneratedAtAsc(symbol, start, end);
 144: 
 145:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET source_signal_count=? WHERE id=?",
 146:                     sourceSignals.size(), runId);
 147:             loadHistoricalDataNs = System.nanoTime() - stageStartedNs;
 148:

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/regression/service/ReplayParityValidator.java:1-140 =====
   1: package com.crypto.regression.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   5: 
   6: import java.util.ArrayList;
   7: import java.util.List;
   8: import java.util.Objects;
   9: 
  10: /**
  11:  * FIX-091 / Fix 6: field-level pre-wallet parity comparator.
  12:  * It deliberately compares trading-intelligence state only; wallet execution is outside scope.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: public class ReplayParityValidator {
  16: 
  17:     public Result compare(TradeSignal production, TradeSignal replay) {
  18:         List<String> mismatches = new ArrayList<>();
  19:         if (production == null || replay == null) {
  20:             mismatches.add("Production and Replay signals must both exist");
  21:             return new Result(false, mismatches);
  22:         }
  23: 
  24:         same(mismatches, "detectedRegime", production.getDetectedRegime(), replay.getDetectedRegime());
  25:         same(mismatches, "candidateRegime", production.getCandidateRegime(), replay.getCandidateRegime());
  26:         same(mismatches, "confirmedRegime", production.getConfirmedRegime(), replay.getConfirmedRegime());
  27:         same(mismatches, "selectedStrategy", production.getSelectedStrategy(), replay.getSelectedStrategy());
  28:         same(mismatches, "originalDecision", production.getOriginalDecision(), replay.getOriginalDecision());
  29:         same(mismatches, "finalDecision", production.getDecision(), replay.getDecision());
  30:         same(mismatches, "rawConfidence", production.getRawConfidenceScore(), replay.getRawConfidenceScore());
  31:         same(mismatches, "effectiveConfidence", production.getEffectiveConfidenceScore(), replay.getEffectiveConfidenceScore());
  32:         same(mismatches, "primaryBlockingStage", production.getPrimaryBlockingStage(), replay.getPrimaryBlockingStage());
  33:         same(mismatches, "btcContextStatus", production.getBtcContextStatus(), replay.getBtcContextStatus());
  34:         same(mismatches, "liquidityStatus", production.getLiquidityStatus(), replay.getLiquidityStatus());
  35:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthority", production.getEntryAuthority(), replay.getEntryAuthority());
  36:         same(mismatches, "entryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent",
  37:                 production.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent(), replay.getEntryAuthorityMaxPositionPercent());
  38:         return new Result(mismatches.isEmpty(), List.copyOf(mismatches));
  39:     }
  40: 
  41:     private void same(List<String> mismatches, String field, Object expected, Object actual) {
  42:         if (!Objects.equals(expected, actual)) {
  43:             mismatches.add(field + ": Production=" + expected + ", Replay=" + actual);
  44:         }
  45:     }
  46: 
  47:     public record Result(boolean matches, List<String> mismatches) {}
  48: }

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/execution/service/ExecutionReplayScope.java:1-240 =====
   1: package com.crypto.execution.service;
   2: 
   3: import com.crypto.domain.TradeSignal;
   4: import com.crypto.execution.domain.ExecutionOpportunity;
   5: import org.springframework.stereotype.Component;
   6: 
   7: import java.time.Instant;
   8: import java.util.ArrayList;
   9: import java.util.Comparator;
  10: import java.util.List;
  11: import java.util.Optional;
  12: import java.util.HashSet;
  13: import java.util.Set;
  14: import java.util.function.Consumer;
  15: 
  16: /**
  17:  * Thread-bound data/persistence override used only by Administration historical replay.
  18:  *
  19:  * The execution algorithms remain the production services. While this scope is active,
  20:  * their signal lookups are resolved exclusively from replay-generated signals as-of the
  21:  * current replay timestamp, and opportunity persistence is redirected to the supplied
  22:  * shadow sink. Production repositories are never read/written by the scoped execution path.
  23:  */
  24: @Component
  25: public class ExecutionReplayScope {
  26: 
  27:     /** FIX-109: Production parity is the default replay contract. Experimental mode is
  28:      * explicit so replay-only candidate rules can never contaminate a parity result. */
  29:     public enum ReplayLogicMode { PRODUCTION_PARITY, EXPERIMENTAL }
  30: 
  31:     private final ThreadLocal<State> state = new ThreadLocal<>();
  32: 
  33:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink) {
  34:         return open(runId, replaySignals, opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY);
  35:     }
  36: 
  37:     public Scope open(long runId, List<TradeSignal> replaySignals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink,
  38:                       ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
  39:         if (state.get() != null) throw new IllegalStateException("Execution replay scope is already active on this thread");
  40:         List<TradeSignal> immutable = replaySignals == null ? List.of() : replaySignals.stream()
  41:                 .filter(s -> s != null && s.getGeneratedAt() != null)
  42:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt))
  43:                 .toList();
  44:         state.set(new State(runId, immutable, opportunitySink, logicMode == null ? ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY : logicMode));
  45:         return new Scope();
  46:     }
  47: 
  48:     /** FIX-122: run-local executed boundaries, independent of Production ledger/history. */
  49:     public void recordStop(String symbol, long positionId, Instant at, String reason) {
  50:         if (!"STOP_LOSS".equals(reason)) return;
  51:         State s=required();
  52:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary previous=s.stops.get(symbol);
  53:         if (previous==null || !at.isBefore(previous.executedAt()))
  54:             s.stops.put(symbol,new StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary(positionId,at));
  55:     }
  56:     public StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary stopBoundary(String symbol, Instant asOf) {
  57:         StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary b=required().stops.get(symbol);
  58:         return b!=null && !b.executedAt().isAfter(asOf) ? b : null;
  59:     }
  60:     public Instant referenceInstant() { return required().reference; }
  61:     public boolean fix122Enabled() { return required().fix122Enabled; }
  62:     public void fix122Enabled(boolean value) { required().fix122Enabled=value; }
  63: 
  64:     public boolean active() { return state.get() != null; }
  65:     public long runId() { return required().runId; }
  66:     public ReplayLogicMode logicMode() { return required().logicMode; }
  67:     public boolean experimental() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.EXPERIMENTAL; }
  68:     public boolean productionParity() { return active() && required().logicMode == ReplayLogicMode.PRODUCTION_PARITY; }
  69: 
  70:     public void reference(Instant reference) { required().reference = reference; }
  71: 
  72:     /** FIX-056: expose the exact latest replay market-price event to the shared execution-price authority. */
  73:     public void referencePrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {
  74:         State s = required();
  75:         s.reference = observedAt;
  76:         s.latestPrice = new ReplayPrice(symbol, observedAt, price);
  77:     }
  78: 
  79:     public Optional<ReplayPrice> latestPrice() {
  80:         State s = required();
  81:         return Optional.ofNullable(s.latestPrice);
  82:     }
  83: 
  84:     /** FIX-112A: Replay-local execution ledger keyed to the exact triggering signal. */
  85:     public boolean entryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  86:         return active() && signalId != null && required().consumedSignalIds.contains(signalId);
  87:     }
  88: 
  89:     /** FIX-112A: record only after that exact shadow BUY (initial or add) executes. */
  90:     public void markEntryConsumed(Long signalId) {
  91:         if (signalId != null) required().consumedSignalIds.add(signalId);
  92:     }
  93: 
  94:     /**
  95:      * Adds a freshly generated replay signal to the as-of context stream. Signal generation
  96:      * uses this incrementally so the production confluence/BTC services can resolve only
  97:      * context that had genuinely been generated by the current replay timestamp.
  98:      */
  99:     public void appendSignal(TradeSignal signal) {
 100:         if (signal == null || signal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 101:         State s = required();
 102:         s.signals.add(signal);
 103:         s.signals.sort(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 104:     }
 105: 
 106:     public List<TradeSignal> recent(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, int limit) {
 107:         State s = required();
 108:         Instant ref = reference != null ? reference : s.reference;
 109:         if (ref == null) return List.of();
 110:         return s.signals.stream()
 111:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 112:                 .filter(x -> !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(ref))
 113:                 .sorted(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt).reversed())
 114:                 .limit(limit)
 115:                 .toList();
 116:     }
 117: 
 118:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 119:         return recent(symbol, interval, reference, 1).stream().findFirst();
 120:     }
 121: 
 122:     /**
 123:      * FIX-141: Replay-side equivalent of
 124:      * TradeSignalRepository#findTopBySymbolAndIntervalAndCandleOpenTimeLessThanEqualAndGeneratedAtLessThanEqualOrderByCandleOpenTimeDesc.
 125:      * Selects by candle open time (close-time ordering), not generatedAt, so Replay cannot diverge
 126:      * from Production on which BTC snapshot it picks. candleOpenTimeBound is the caller's look-ahead
 127:      * bound (reference minus the interval duration); generatedAt must also be at or before reference.
 128:      */
 129:     public Optional<TradeSignal> latestClosedAtOrBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference, Instant candleOpenTimeBound) {
 130:         State s = required();
 131:         if (reference == null || candleOpenTimeBound == null) return Optional.empty();
 132:         return s.signals.stream()
 133:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 134:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt() != null && !x.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference))
 135:                 .filter(x -> x.getCandleOpenTime() != null && !x.getCandleOpenTime().isAfter(candleOpenTimeBound))
 136:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getCandleOpenTime));
 137:     }
 138: 
 139:     public Optional<TradeSignal> previousBefore(String symbol, String interval, Instant reference) {
 140:         State s = required();
 141:         return s.signals.stream()
 142:                 .filter(x -> symbol.equals(x.getSymbol()) && interval.equals(x.getInterval()))
 143:                 .filter(x -> x.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference))
 144:                 .max(Comparator.comparing(TradeSignal::getGeneratedAt));
 145:     }
 146: 
 147:     public Optional<ExecutionOpportunity> currentOpportunity(String symbol, List<String> statuses) {
 148:         ExecutionOpportunity o = required().opportunity;
 149:         if (o == null || !symbol.equals(o.getSymbol())) return Optional.empty();
 150:         if (statuses != null && !statuses.isEmpty() && !statuses.contains(o.getStatus())) return Optional.empty();
 151:         return Optional.of(o);
 152:     }
 153: 
 154:     public ExecutionOpportunity saveOpportunity(ExecutionOpportunity opportunity) {
 155:         State s = required();
 156:         s.opportunity = opportunity;
 157:         if (s.opportunitySink != null) s.opportunitySink.accept(opportunity);
 158:         return opportunity;
 159:     }
 160: 
 161:     public final class Scope implements AutoCloseable {
 162:         private boolean closed;
 163:         @Override public void close() {
 164:             if (!closed) {
 165:                 state.remove();
 166:                 closed = true;
 167:             }
 168:         }
 169:     }
 170: 
 171:     private State required() {
 172:         State s = state.get();
 173:         if (s == null) throw new IllegalStateException("No Administration execution replay scope is active");
 174:         return s;
 175:     }
 176: 
 177:     public record ReplayPrice(String symbol, Instant observedAt, java.math.BigDecimal price) {}
 178: 
 179:     private static final class State {
 180:         final long runId;
 181:         final List<TradeSignal> signals;
 182:         final Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink;
 183:         final ReplayLogicMode logicMode;
 184:         boolean fix122Enabled = true;
 185:         final java.util.Map<String,StopLossEvidencePolicy.Boundary> stops = new java.util.HashMap<>();
 186:         Instant reference;
 187:         ExecutionOpportunity opportunity;
 188:         ReplayPrice latestPrice;
 189:         final Set<Long> consumedSignalIds = new HashSet<>();
 190:         State(long runId, List<TradeSignal> signals, Consumer<ExecutionOpportunity> opportunitySink, ReplayLogicMode logicMode) {
 191:             this.runId = runId;
 192:             this.signals = new ArrayList<>(signals);
 193:             this.opportunitySink = opportunitySink;
 194:             this.logicMode = logicMode;
 195:         }
 196:     }

 421:                     SET status='ERROR', progress_percent=100, current_step='Regression test failed', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6),
 422:                         error_message=?, failure_step=?, failure_exception=?, failure_root_cause=?,
 423:                         failure_stack_trace=?, started_at=COALESCE(started_at, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)),
 424:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 425:                     WHERE id=?
 426:                     """,
 427:                     summary, failedStep, exception.getClass().getName(),
 428:                     rootCauseDetail(root, 1000), stackTrace(exception), runId);
 429:         } finally {
 430:             // FIX-11J: persist one timing snapshot after the existing Replay path has completed or
 431:             // unwound. Diagnostics persistence is deliberately best-effort: it must never replace
 432:             // the Replay's real PASSED/FAILED/ERROR outcome or swallow the original exception.
 433:             long replayTotalNs = System.nanoTime() - replayTotalStartedNs;

 438:             // FIX-087: always release ownership, including ERROR paths, so a genuinely stopped
 439:             // replay remains recoverable later using the same run id and original replay window.
 440:             activeRunIds.remove(runId);
 441:             stopRequestedRunIds.remove(runId);
 442:         }

 445:     private void persistReplayTimingsSafely(long runId, Long loadHistoricalDataNs,
 446:                                             Long verifyEventResolutionNs, Long buildReplayDatasetNs,
 447:                                             Long generateFreshSignalsNs, Long shadowExecutionNs,
 448:                                             Long parityComparisonNs, long replayTotalNs) {
 449:         try {
 450:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 451:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 452:                     SET timing_load_historical_ns=?, timing_verify_event_resolution_ns=?,
 453:                         timing_build_replay_dataset_ns=?, timing_generate_fresh_signals_ns=?,
 454:                         timing_shadow_execution_ns=?, timing_parity_comparison_ns=?, timing_total_ns=?
 455:                     WHERE id=?
 456:                     """, loadHistoricalDataNs, verifyEventResolutionNs, buildReplayDatasetNs,
 457:                     generateFreshSignalsNs, shadowExecutionNs, parityComparisonNs, replayTotalNs, runId);
 458:         } catch (Exception timingPersistenceFailure) {
 459:             log.error("FIX-11J: unable to persist Replay timing diagnostics for run {}",
 460:                     runId, timingPersistenceFailure);
 461:         }
 462:     }
 463: 
 464:     /**
 465:      * FIX-11H: loads one immutable replay dataset. The prefix comes from the exact existing
 466:      * closed/as-of query, so missing candle gaps cannot shorten history. The forward segment
 467:      * explicitly requires closed=true. No Production caller reaches this method.
 468:      */
 469:     private ReplayDataset loadDataset(String symbol, String btcSymbol, Instant start, Instant end) {
 470:         Map<ReplayDataset.CandleKey, List<Candle>> candleMap = new HashMap<>();
 471:         Set<String> symbols = new LinkedHashSet<>(List.of(symbol, btcSymbol));
 472:         int historyLimit = technicalIndicatorService.regressionHistoryLimit();
 473:         for (String sym : symbols) {
 474:             for (String interval : List.of("1m", "5m", "1h")) {
 475:                 List<Candle> prefix = new java.util.ArrayList<>(candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 476:                         sym, interval, start, PageRequest.of(0, historyLimit)));
 477:                 java.util.Collections.reverse(prefix);
 478:                 List<Candle> forward = candleRepository
 479:                         .findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndClosedTrueAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 480:                                 sym, interval, start, end);
 481:                 List<Candle> merged = new java.util.ArrayList<>(prefix.size() + forward.size());
 482:                 merged.addAll(prefix);
 483:                 Instant prefixEnd = prefix.isEmpty() ? Instant.EPOCH : prefix.get(prefix.size() - 1).getOpenTime();
 484:                 forward.stream().filter(c -> c.getOpenTime().isAfter(prefixEnd)).forEach(merged::add);
 485:                 candleMap.put(new ReplayDataset.CandleKey(sym, interval), merged);
 486:                 log.info("FIX-11H dataset candles: symbol={}, interval={}, prefix={}, forward={}, merged={}",
 487:                         sym, interval, prefix.size(), forward.size(), merged.size());
 488:             }
 489:         }
 490: 
 491:         Map<ReplayDataset.SignalKey, Long> lineage = new HashMap<>();
 492:         // FIX-11H / FIX-112D: lineage is candle identity, not signal persistence timing.
 493:         // Recovery/backfilled Production signals can be generated hours after their candle, so
 494:         // generatedAt MUST NOT define this preload window. Select and match lineage by the same
 495:         // exact candleOpenTime identity used by ReplayDataset.sourceSignalId().
 496:         for (TradeSignal signal : signalRepository.findBySymbolAndCandleOpenTimeBetweenOrderByCandleOpenTimeAsc(symbol, start, end)) {
 497:             ReplayDataset.SignalKey key = new ReplayDataset.SignalKey(
 498:                     signal.getSymbol(), signal.getInterval(), signal.getCandleOpenTime());
 499:             Long previous = lineage.putIfAbsent(key, signal.getId());
 500:             if (previous != null && !previous.equals(signal.getId())) {
 501:                 throw new IllegalStateException("Replay lineage invariant violated for " + key
 502:                         + ": signalIds=" + previous + "," + signal.getId());
 503:             }
 504:         }
 505:         log.info("FIX-11H dataset lineage: symbol={}, entries={}", symbol, lineage.size());
 506:         return new ReplayDataset(candleMap, lineage);
 507:     }
 508: 
 509:     private List<Candle> candles(String symbol, String interval, Instant start, Instant end) {
 510:         return candleRepository.findBySymbolAndIntervalCodeAndOpenTimeBetweenOrderByOpenTimeAsc(
 511:                 symbol, interval, start, end);
 512:     }
 513: 
 514:     private int verifyEventResolution(long runId, String symbol, String interval, List<Candle> candles,
 515:                                       int progressStart, int progressEnd) {
 516:         int resolved = 0;
 517:         int size = candles.size();
 518:         for (int i = 0; i < size; i++) {
 519:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 520:             Candle candle = candles.get(i);
 521:             List<Candle> latestAtEvent = candleRepository.findClosedCandlesAtOrBefore(
 522:                     symbol, interval, candle.getOpenTime(), PageRequest.of(0, 1));
 523:             if (!latestAtEvent.isEmpty() && candle.getOpenTime().equals(latestAtEvent.get(0).getOpenTime())) {
 524:                 resolved++;
 525:             }
 526:             if (i % 25 == 0 && size > 0) {
 527:                 int p = progressStart + (int) Math.round((progressEnd - progressStart) * (i / (double) size));
 528:                 updateRun(runId, "RUNNING", p,
 529:                         "Replaying " + interval + " candle " + (i + 1) + "/" + size, null);
 530:             }
 531:         }
 532:         return resolved;
 533:     }
 534: 
 535:     private FreshReplayStats generateFreshSignals(
 536:             long runId,
 537:             String symbol,
 538:             Instant executionStart,
 539:             Instant executionEnd,
 540:             List<Candle> oneMinuteCandles,
 541:             List<Candle> fiveMinuteCandles,
 542:             List<Candle> oneHourCandles,
 543:             String btcSymbol,
 544:             List<Candle> btcOneMinuteCandles,
 545:             List<Candle> btcFiveMinuteCandles,
 546:             List<Candle> btcOneHourCandles,
 547:             ReplayDataSource dataSource,
 548:             ReplayDataset replayDataset
 549:     ) {
 550:         List<ReplayCandle> timeline = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 551:         oneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1m", c, true)));
 552:         fiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "5m", c, true)));
 553:         oneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(symbol, "1h", c, true)));
 554:         btcOneMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1m", c, false)));
 555:         btcFiveMinuteCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "5m", c, false)));
 556:         btcOneHourCandles.forEach(c -> timeline.add(new ReplayCandle(btcSymbol, "1h", c, false)));
 557:         timeline.sort(java.util.Comparator
 558:                 .comparing((ReplayCandle rc) -> rc.candle().getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(rc.interval())))
 559:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> intervalOrder(rc.interval()))
 560:                 .thenComparingInt(rc -> rc.primary() ? 1 : 0));
 561: 
 562:         int oneMinuteSignals = 0, fiveMinuteSignals = 0, oneHourSignals = 0;
 563:         int oneMinuteBuys = 0, fiveMinuteBuys = 0, oneHourBuys = 0;
 564:         int buys = 0, watches = 0, sells = 0, strongSells = 0, errors = 0;
 565:         java.util.List<TradeSignal> generatedSignals = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
 566: 
 567:         // FIX-11L: Replay-only deep profiling for the Generate fresh signals stage.
 568:         // These monotonic timers observe existing calls only; they do not alter inputs,
 569:         // call ordering, persistence, thresholds, or any Production trading behavior.
 570:         ReplayFreshSignalProfiler profiler = new ReplayFreshSignalProfiler(runId, dataSource);
 571:         // FIX-11M: Enable aggregate timing only for this Replay worker thread.
 572:         // The shared AnalysisService decision path is unchanged; this is observation-only.
 573:         analysisService.beginReplayAnalysisProfiling(runId);
 574:         try (ExecutionReplayScope.Scope ignored = replayScope.open(runId, List.of(), o -> {})) {
 575:         // FIX-043 Replay parity: fresh Replay has always generated technical snapshots/signals
 576:         // chronologically for EVERY closed candle in the timeline. Production now restores the
 577:         // same no-gap contract with asynchronous close-event processing plus chronological recovery.
 578:         // Do not "optimize" Replay by sampling every Nth candle or by copying the production recovery
 579:         // scheduler cadence; that would recreate the exact 1m->~5m blind-gap incident FIX-043 fixes.
 580:         for (int index = 0; index < timeline.size(); index++) {
 581:             checkStopRequested(runId);
 582:             ReplayCandle replay = timeline.get(index);
 583:             Candle candle = replay.candle();
 584:             Instant evaluationTime = candle.getOpenTime().plusSeconds(intervalSeconds(replay.interval()));
 585:             boolean inRequestedWindow = !evaluationTime.isBefore(executionStart) && !evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd);
 586:             try {
 587:                 long operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 588:                 java.util.Optional<IndicatorSnapshot> snapshot;
 589:                 try {
 590:                     snapshot = dataSource == ReplayDataSource.DATASET
 591:                             ? technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 592:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime(), replayDataset)
 593:                             : technicalIndicatorService.calculateSnapshotForRegression(
 594:                                     replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle.getOpenTime());
 595:                 } finally {
 596:                     profiler.recordSnapshot(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 597:                 }
 598:                 if (snapshot.isEmpty()) {
 599:                     if (inRequestedWindow) {
 600:                         errors++;
 601:                         saveGenerationError(runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), candle,
 602:                                 "Not enough historical candles to calculate the technical snapshot");
 603:                     }
 604:                     continue;
 605:                 }
 606: 
 607:                 // Do not generate a signal whose candle would only have closed after the
 608:                 // requested test end. Warm-up rows before executionStart are intentionally
 609:                 // generated for as-of context, but are not counted or displayed as test output.
 610:                 if (evaluationTime.isAfter(executionEnd)) continue;
 611: 
 612:                 replayScope.reference(evaluationTime);
 613:                 operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 614:                 TradeSignal generated;
 615:                 try {
 616:                     generated = analysisService.analyzeForRegression(snapshot.get(), evaluationTime);
 617:                 } finally {
 618:                     profiler.recordAnalysis(System.nanoTime() - operationStartedNs);
 619:                 }
 620:                 replayScope.appendSignal(generated);
 621:                 SignalDecision decision = generated.getDecision();
 622:                 if (replay.primary()) generatedSignals.add(generated);
 623: 
 624:                 if (replay.primary() && inRequestedWindow) {
 625:                     if ("1m".equals(replay.interval())) { oneMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneMinuteBuys++; }
 626:                     else if ("5m".equals(replay.interval())) { fiveMinuteSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) fiveMinuteBuys++; }
 627:                     else if ("1h".equals(replay.interval())) { oneHourSignals++; if (isBuy(decision)) oneHourBuys++; }
 628: 
 629:                     if (decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY) buys++;
 630:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.WATCH) watches++;
 631:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.SELL) sells++;
 632:                     else if (decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL) strongSells++;
 633: 
 634:                     operationStartedNs = System.nanoTime();
 635:                     try {
 636:                         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 637:                             INSERT INTO analysis_test_signal
 638:                                 (test_run_id, source_signal_id, replay_generated, symbol, interval_code, candle_open_time,
 639:                                  generated_at, latest_price, original_decision, final_decision,
 640:                                  execution_effective_decision, total_score, confidence_score, raw_confidence_score,
 641:                                  effective_confidence_score, primary_blocking_stage, detected_regime, candidate_regime,
 642:                                  confirmed_regime, regime_candidate_count, entry_authority, entry_authority_max_position_percent,
 643:                                  trend_score, volume_score, momentum_score, decision_authority_corrected, generation_error)
 644:                             VALUES (?, NULL, 1, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 0, NULL)
 645:                             """,
 646:                             runId, replay.symbol(), replay.interval(), Timestamp.from(candle.getOpenTime()),
 647:                             Timestamp.from(evaluationTime), generated.getLatestPrice(),
 648:                             generated.getOriginalDecision() == null ? null : generated.getOriginalDecision().name(),
 649:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),
 650:                             decision == null ? null : decision.name(),

  74:     @Transactional
  75:     public void onPrice(String symbolValue, BigDecimal price) {

 203:                             "progressPct={}, activationPct={}, lock={}, state={}, triggered={}",
 204:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, managed.getTakeProfitUsdt(), lock.highestPrice(), lock.active(),
 205:                     lock.progressPercent(), lock.activationPercent(), lock.lockPrice(), lock.state(), lock.triggered());
 206:         }
 207:         if (lock.triggered()) {

 209:                     lock.walletPositionId(), symbol, price, lock.lockPrice(), lock.progressPercent(), managed.getTakeProfitUsdt());

 231:         }

 234:         // failure can therefore close a winner instead of waiting forever for an exact 1m SELL event.
 235:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(one, five, hour);
 236:         if (normalExit.exit()) {

 238:                 closePaper(symbol, price, PositionStatus.CLOSED, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation(), one);

 240:             }
 241:             // FIX-11T: any higher-priority position action owns this evaluation cycle.
 242:             // Near-TP must never race another SELL or quantity-changing decision on the same tick.
 243:             return;
 244:         }

 247:         // existing signal exits above retain their exact authority and always win the cycle.
 248:         evaluateNearTpProtection(managed, price, one, five);
 249:     }

 711:     private ShadowPosition updateProfitLock(ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s) {
 712:         if (s.getLatestPrice() == null || !priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime())) return p;
 713:         ShadowPosition n = profitLockState(p, s.getLatestPrice());
 714:         if (!equalsNullable(p.highest(), n.highest()) || p.profitLockActive() != n.profitLockActive()
 715:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockPrice())
 716:                 || p.profitLockState() != n.profitLockState()
 717:                 || !java.util.Objects.equals(p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt(), n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt())) {
 718:             jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET highest_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_active=?, profit_lock_price_usdt=?, profit_lock_state=?, profit_lock_rebase_started_at=? WHERE id=?",
 719:                     n.highest(), n.profitLockActive(), n.profitLockPrice(), n.profitLockState().name(),
 720:                     n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt() == null ? null : Timestamp.from(n.profitLockRebaseStartedAt()), p.positionId());
 721:         }
 722:         return n;
 723:     }

 725:     private ShadowPosition profitLockState(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal price) {
 726:         WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 727:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(settings.getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 728:         BigDecimal highest = p.highest() == null ? price : p.highest().max(price);
 729:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), highest);
 730: 
 731:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 732:                 && progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 733:             // Replay parity with Production FIX-118: preserve the historical lock/legacy flag,
 734:             // but keep it non-executable until the new TP geometry earns activation again.
 735:             return p.withLock(highest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 736:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 737:         }
 738: 
 739:         // FIX-118 parity: when REBASE earns activation, rebuild the lock from the current
 740:         // TP geometry instead of retaining the superseded pre-extension lock.
 741:         boolean freshGeometryActivation = p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE;
 742:         ProfitLockPolicy.State state = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 743:                 p.entryPrice(), p.takeProfit(), price, highest,
 744:                 freshGeometryActivation ? false : (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE || p.profitLockActive()),
 745:                 freshGeometryActivation ? null : p.profitLockPrice(),
 746:                 settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 747:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 748:                 nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 749:         ProfitLockState nextState = state.active() ? ProfitLockState.ACTIVE : ProfitLockState.INACTIVE;
 750:         return p.withLock(state.highestPrice(), state.active(), state.lockPrice(), nextState, null);
 751:     }
 752: 
 753:     private ShadowPosition rebaseForExtendedTarget(ShadowPosition p, BigDecimal newTarget, BigDecimal extensionPrice, Instant at) {
 754:         if (p == null || newTarget == null) return p;
 755:         BigDecimal oldTarget = p.takeProfit();
 756:         BigDecimal oldLock = p.profitLockPrice();
 757:         BigDecimal activation = nvl(walletSettings().getProfitLockActivationPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(70));
 758:         BigDecimal extensionHighest = p.highest() == null ? p.entryPrice() : p.highest();
 759:         if (extensionPrice != null && extensionPrice.compareTo(extensionHighest) > 0) extensionHighest = extensionPrice;
 760:         ShadowPosition withExtensionHigh = p.withLock(extensionHighest, p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(),
 761:                 p.profitLockState(), p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 762:         BigDecimal progress = progressPercent(p.entryPrice(), newTarget, extensionHighest);
 763:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE) {
 764:             if (progress.compareTo(activation) < 0) {
 765:                 ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 766:                         at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 767:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 768:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 769:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 770:                         extensionHighest, progress, oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 771:                 return rebasing;
 772:             }
 773:             // FIX-120 Production/Replay parity: the old executable lock belongs to the
 774:             // superseded TP geometry. If the extended winner still qualifies, establish a
 775:             // fresh geometry baseline. Raw extensionPrice is current; extensionHighest is the
 776:             // historical-best seed; previousActive/previousLock are deliberately discarded.
 777:             WalletSettings settings = walletSettings();
 778:             BigDecimal currentForPolicy = extensionPrice == null ? extensionHighest : extensionPrice;
 779:             ProfitLockPolicy.State rebased = profitLockPolicy.evaluate(
 780:                     p.entryPrice(), newTarget, currentForPolicy, extensionHighest,
 781:                     false, null, settings.isDynamicProfitLockEnabled(), activation,
 782:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockInitialPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(40)),
 783:                     nvl(settings.getProfitLockTrailStepPercent(), BigDecimal.valueOf(10)));
 784:             if (rebased.active()) {
 785:                 ShadowPosition active = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, rebased.lockPrice(), ProfitLockState.ACTIVE, null);
 786:                 log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 787:                                 "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 788:                         p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), active.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 789:                         rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebased.lockPrice(), activation);
 790:                 return active;
 791:             }
 792:             ShadowPosition rebasing = withExtensionHigh.withLock(rebased.highestPrice(), true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 793:                     at == null ? Instant.now() : at);
 794:             log.info("[FIX-120][REPLAY][TP_GEOMETRY_REBASE] positionId={}, previousState={}, newState={}, oldTarget={}, newTarget={}, " +
 795:                             "extensionPrice={}, highest={}, newGeometryProgressPct={}, oldLock={}, freshLock={}, activationPct={}, geometryRebased=true",
 796:                     p.positionId(), p.profitLockState(), rebasing.profitLockState(), oldTarget, newTarget, extensionPrice,
 797:                     rebased.highestPrice(), rebased.progressPercent(), oldLock, rebasing.profitLockPrice(), activation);
 798:             return rebasing;
 799:         }
 800:         if (p.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE) {
 801:             return withExtensionHigh.withLock(extensionHighest, true, p.profitLockPrice(), ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE,
 802:                     p.profitLockRebaseStartedAt());
 803:         }
 804:         return withExtensionHigh;
 805:     }

 807:     private BigDecimal progressPercent(BigDecimal entry, BigDecimal target, BigDecimal highest) {
 808:         if (entry == null || target == null || highest == null || target.compareTo(entry) <= 0) return BigDecimal.ZERO;
 809:         BigDecimal favorable = highest.subtract(entry);
 810:         if (favorable.signum() < 0) favorable = BigDecimal.ZERO;
 811:         return favorable.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 812:                 .divide(target.subtract(entry, MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 813:     }

 841:     private String replayStage(int allocationPercent) {
 842:         if (allocationPercent <= 0) return "NONE";
 843:         if (allocationPercent < 50) return "SCOUT_ENTRY";
 844:         if (allocationPercent < 100) return "CONFIRMATION_ADD";
 845:         return "TREND_ADD";
 846:     }

 848:     private ShadowPosition addToPosition(long runId, ShadowPosition open, TradeSignal signal,
 849:                                          BigDecimal addedQty, BigDecimal addedCost, int addPercent) {
 850:         BigDecimal newQuantity = open.quantity().add(addedQty, MC);
 851:         BigDecimal newCost = open.cost().add(addedCost, MC);
 852:         BigDecimal newEntry = newCost.divide(newQuantity, 12, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 853:         BigDecimal newStop = open.stopLoss();
 854:         if (signal.getStopLoss() != null && (newStop == null || signal.getStopLoss().compareTo(newStop) > 0)) {
 855:             newStop = signal.getStopLoss();
 856:         }
 857:         BigDecimal newTakeProfit = open.takeProfit();
 858:         if (signal.getTakeProfit() != null && (newTakeProfit == null || signal.getTakeProfit().compareTo(newTakeProfit) > 0)) {
 859:             newTakeProfit = signal.getTakeProfit();
 860:         }
 861:         int newPercent = Math.min(100, open.positionPercent() + addPercent);
 862:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 863:                 UPDATE wallet_position_test
 864:                 SET entry_price=?, quantity=?, total_cost_usdt=?, position_percent=?,
 865:                     stop_loss_usdt=?, take_profit_usdt=?
 866:                 WHERE id=? AND test_run_id=?
 867:                 """, newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit,
 868:                 open.positionId(), runId);
 869:         ShadowPosition updated = open.withAdd(newEntry, newQuantity, newCost, newPercent, newStop, newTakeProfit);
 870:         persistNearTpState(updated);
 871:         return updated;
 872:     }

 562:     private ExitDecision evaluateExit(long runId, ShadowPosition p, TradeSignal s, TradeSignal oneMinute, TradeSignal five, TradeSignal one) {
 563:         BigDecimal price = s.getLatestPrice();
 564:         if (price == null) return ExitDecision.hold();
 565: 
 566:         // Replay uses the exact same temporal price-authority rule as production. A signal
 567:         // generated later may still carry a candle close from before entry; that historical
 568:         // price may update MTF context but may not retroactively hit TP/SL/profit-lock.
 569:         boolean authoritativePrice = priceAuthorityPolicy.canUseSignalPrice(s, p.entryTime());
 570:         if (authoritativePrice && p.takeProfit() != null && price.compareTo(p.takeProfit()) >= 0) {
 571:             // Exact production TP-continuation call, including immutable entry structure.
 572:             // FIX-011 regression relies on Replay and live using this same shared policy.
 573:             PositionContinuationPolicy.Evaluation continuation = continuationPolicy.evaluate(
 574:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 575:                     p.entryTrend(), p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(),
 576:                     p.entryConfidence(), p.entryScore());
 577:             if (continuation.extendTarget()) {
 578:                 BigDecimal distance = p.takeProfit().subtract(p.entryPrice());
 579:                 BigDecimal newTarget = p.takeProfit().add(distance.multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC), MC);
 580:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXTENDED", p.takeProfit(), newTarget, p, continuation.explanation());
 581:                 jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE wallet_position_test SET take_profit_usdt=? WHERE id=?", newTarget, p.positionId());
 582:                 return new ExitDecision(false, "EXTEND_TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation(), newTarget);
 583:             }
 584:             oneCandleContinuationGraceReplayObserver.observeBaselineTakeProfitFailure(
 585:                     runId, s.getSymbol(), p.positionId(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 586:                     p.entryTime(), p.entryPrice(), p.quantity(), p.cost(), p.stopLoss(), p.takeProfit(), p.highest(),
 587:                     p.profitLockActive(), p.profitLockPrice(), p.entryScore(), p.entryConfidence(), p.entryTrend(),
 588:                     p.entryStructure(), p.entryMomentum(), p.entryVolume(), oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one,
 589:                     continuation.explanation());
 590:             persistManagement(runId, s, "TAKE_PROFIT_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), p, continuation.explanation());
 591:             return new ExitDecision(true, "TAKE_PROFIT", continuation.explanation());
 592:         }
 593:         // FIX-052 fallback ordering also mirrors Production: STOP_LOSS is absolute and
 594:         // is evaluated before profit-lock. Exact V64 runs normally take this path from
 595:         // persisted live-price events rather than from signal-carried prices.
 596:         if (authoritativePrice && p.stopLoss() != null && price.compareTo(p.stopLoss()) <= 0)
 597:             return new ExitDecision(true, "STOP_LOSS", "Price reached the stored stop loss.");
 598: 
 599:         ShadowPosition updated = authoritativePrice ? profitLockState(p, price) : p;
 600:         if (authoritativePrice && (updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE
 601:                 || updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 602:                 || p.profitLockActive() != updated.profitLockActive()
 603:                 || !equalsNullable(p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice())
 604:                 || p.profitLockState() != updated.profitLockState())) {
 605:             BigDecimal fix118Progress = updated.takeProfit() == null || updated.takeProfit().compareTo(updated.entryPrice()) <= 0
 606:                     ? BigDecimal.ZERO
 607:                     : updated.highest().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC)
 608:                             .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100), MC)
 609:                             .divide(updated.takeProfit().subtract(updated.entryPrice(), MC), 6, RoundingMode.HALF_UP);
 610:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_PATH] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, " +
 611:                             "entry={}, target={}, highest={}, previousActive={}, active={}, previousState={}, state={}, previousLock={}, lock={}, progressPct={}, triggered={}",
 612:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 613:                     updated.entryPrice(), updated.takeProfit(), updated.highest(), p.profitLockActive(), updated.profitLockActive(),
 614:                     p.profitLockState(), updated.profitLockState(), p.profitLockPrice(), updated.profitLockPrice(), fix118Progress,
 615:                     updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 616:                             && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0);
 617:         }
 618:         BigDecimal minimumProfitableExit = p.entryPrice().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(1.0005));
 619:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.TP_EXTENSION_REBASE
 620:                 && price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 621:             String floorReason = "TP-extension rebase safety floor was breached at " + minimumProfitableExit + ". "
 622:                     + profitLockConfigText();
 623:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_REBASE_HARD_FLOOR] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, floor={}",
 624:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price, minimumProfitableExit);
 625:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 626:             return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 627:         }
 628:         if (authoritativePrice && updated.profitLockState() == ProfitLockState.ACTIVE && updated.profitLockPrice() != null
 629:                 && price.compareTo(updated.profitLockPrice()) <= 0) {
 630:             log.info("[FIX-118][REPLAY][SIGNAL_TRIGGER] runId={}, positionId={}, signalId={}, symbol={}, generatedAt={}, price={}, lock={}, target={}",
 631:                     runId, updated.positionId(), s.getId(), s.getSymbol(), s.getGeneratedAt(), price,
 632:                     updated.profitLockPrice(), updated.takeProfit());
 633:             if (price.compareTo(minimumProfitableExit) < 0) {
 634:                 String floorReason = "Profit-lock hard floor was breached; protected profit can no longer be preserved. "
 635:                         + profitLockConfigText();
 636:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, floorReason);
 637:                 return new ExitDecision(true, "PROFIT_LOCK_HARD_EXIT", floorReason);
 638:             }
 639:             PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation lockDecision = exitPolicy.evaluateProfitLockBreach(
 640:                     oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 641:             if (!lockDecision.exit()) {
 642:                 String holdReason = lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 643:                 persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_HOLD", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, holdReason);
 644:                 return ExitDecision.hold();
 645:             }
 646:             String lockReason = "Price retraced to the protected profit level after a profitable advance. "
 647:                     + lockDecision.explanation() + " " + profitLockConfigText();
 648:             persistManagement(runId, s, "PROFIT_LOCK_EXIT", p.takeProfit(), p.takeProfit(), updated, lockReason);
 649:             return new ExitDecision(true, lockDecision.code(), lockReason);
 650:         }
 651:         // Production has two SELL authorities: the live HTF PositionExitPolicy and the
 652:         // 1m signal TradeExecutionValidationService. Replay executes both shared production
 653:         // policies in the same order instead of carrying a test-only SELL rule.
 654:         PositionExitPolicy.Evaluation normalExit = exitPolicy.evaluateNormalExit(
 655:                 oneMinute != null ? oneMinute : s, five, one);
 656:         if (normalExit.exit()) {
 657:             return new ExitDecision(true, normalExit.code(), normalExit.explanation());
 658:         }
 659:         if ("1m".equals(s.getInterval()) && bearish(s.getDecision())) {
 660:             TradeExecutionValidationService.ValidationResult validatedSell = executionValidationService.validateSell(s);
 661:             if (validatedSell.allowed()) {
 662:                 return new ExitDecision(true, validatedSell.code(), validatedSell.explanation());
 663:             }
 664:         }
 665:         return ExitDecision.hold();
 666:     }

 668:     private FallbackNearTpEvaluation evaluateNearTpAtSignalFallback(long runId, ShadowPosition current,
 669:                                                                     TradeSignal signal, TradeSignal oneMinute,
 670:                                                                     TradeSignal fiveMinute) {
 671:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State state = new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 672:                 current.nearTpState(), current.nearTpBestPrice(), current.nearTpBearishStreak(),
 673:                 current.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), current.nearTpHarvestUsed());
 674:         NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.Evaluation evaluation = nearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.evaluate(
 675:                 state, current.entryPrice(), current.takeProfit(), signal.getLatestPrice(), signal.getGeneratedAt(),
 676:                 oneMinute, fiveMinute);
 677:         ShadowPosition updated = current.withNearTp(evaluation.state());
 678:         if (!updated.sameNearTp(current)) persistNearTpState(updated);
 679:         if (evaluation.transition()) {
 680:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback Near-TP: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, code={}, state={}, price={}, givebackPct={}, bearish1mStreak={}, detail={}",
 681:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), evaluation.code(), updated.nearTpState(),
 682:                     signal.getLatestPrice(), evaluation.givebackPercent(), updated.nearTpBearishStreak(), evaluation.explanation());
 683:         }
 684:         if (!evaluation.harvestEligible()) return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 685: 
 686:         BigDecimal soldQty = updated.quantity().multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(0.50), MC);
 687:         BinanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.Evaluation minimumCheck =
 688:                 binanceMinimumExecutionPolicy.evaluate(signal.getSymbol(), soldQty, signal.getLatestPrice());
 689:         if (!minimumCheck.executable()) {
 690:             updated = updated.withNearTp(new NearTpFailureProtectionPolicy.State(
 691:                     NearTpState.NEAR_TP_REJECTION_DETECTED, updated.nearTpBestPrice(),
 692:                     0, updated.nearTpLastOneMinuteSignalId(), false));
 693:             persistNearTpState(updated);
 694:             log.info("FIX-11T Replay fallback NEAR_TP_HARVEST_SKIPPED: runId={}, positionId={}, symbol={}, reason={}, requestedQty={}, requestedNotional={}, binanceMinimum={}; existing position management continues",
 695:                     runId, updated.positionId(), signal.getSymbol(), minimumCheck.code(), soldQty,
 696:                     minimumCheck.requestedNotional(), minimumCheck.minimumNotional());
 697:             return new FallbackNearTpEvaluation(updated, BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal.ZERO);
 698:         }
 699:         BigDecimal proceeds = soldQty.multiply(signal.getLatestPrice(), MC);
 700:         BigDecimal soldCost = updated.entryPrice().multiply(soldQty, MC);
 701:         BigDecimal pnl = proceeds.subtract(soldCost, MC);
 702:         BigDecimal pnlPct = soldCost.signum() == 0 ? BigDecimal.ZERO : pnl.divide(soldCost, 8, RoundingMode.HALF_UP)
 703:                 .multiply(BigDecimal.valueOf(100));

  45:     // FIX-11G: this mirrors the existing global CHASE_ENTRY_CUTOFF in
  46:     // ExecutionIntelligenceService. We deliberately reuse the proven execution-quality
  47:     // boundary instead of inventing a new confidence/quality threshold for this one route.
  48:     // The downstream global Entry Quality guard still remains authoritative and re-checks it.
  49:     private static final int BALANCED_TRANSITIONAL_MIN_ENTRY_QUALITY = 50;
  50:

  62:     public ValidationResult validateBuy(TradeSignal executionSignal, int entryQualityScore) {
  63:         ValidationResult structural = validateBaseSignal(executionSignal, true);
  64:         if (!structural.allowed()) return structural;
  65: 
  66:         WalletSettings settings = settings();
  67:         if (settings.isRequireNewBuyTransition()) {
  68:             TradeSignal previous = previousSignal(executionSignal.getSymbol(), EXECUTION_INTERVAL, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt()).orElse(null);
  69:             // FIX-112A: a bullish label alone is not a consumed entry. Only keep
  70:             // BUY_CONTINUATION protection when that exact previous signal has an
  71:             // executed BUY (Production) or executed shadow BUY (Replay).
  72:             if (isFresh(previous, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_MINUTE_TRANSITION_MAX_AGE)
  73:                     && isBullish(previous.getDecision())
  74:                     && entryConsumptionPolicy.resolve(previous.getId()) != EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED) {
  75:                 return ValidationResult.reject("BUY_CONTINUATION",
  76:                         "BUY was not executed because a recent bullish opportunity for this symbol was already consumed. "
  77:                                 + "A new WATCH/NEUTRAL/SELL -> BUY transition is required.");
  78:             }
  79:         }
  80: 
  81:         TradeSignal fiveMinute = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
  82:         if (!isFresh(fiveMinute, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), FIVE_MINUTE_MAX_AGE)) {
  83:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_5M_CONFIRMATION",
  84:                     "No fresh 5m confirmation was available for the 1m BUY signal.");
  85:         }
  86: 
  87:         TradeSignal oneHour = latestAtOrBefore(executionSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);
  88:         if (!isFresh(oneHour, executionSignal.getGeneratedAt(), ONE_HOUR_MAX_AGE)) {
  89:             return ValidationResult.reject("MISSING_1H_CONTEXT",
  90:                     "No fresh 1h strategic context was available for the 1m BUY signal.");

===== src/main/java/com/crypto/service/TradeExecutionValidationService.java =====
 380: 
 381:     private boolean isFresh(TradeSignal signal, Instant reference, Duration maximumAge) {
 382:         return signal != null
 383:                 && signal.getGeneratedAt() != null
 384:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isAfter(reference)
 385:                 && !signal.getGeneratedAt().isBefore(reference.minus(maximumAge));
 386:     }
 387: 
 388:     private boolean isBullish(SignalDecision decision) {
 389:         return decision == SignalDecision.BUY || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_BUY;
 390:     }
 391: 
 392:     private boolean isBearish(SignalDecision decision) {
 393:         return decision == SignalDecision.SELL || decision == SignalDecision.STRONG_SELL;
 394:     }
 395: 
 396:     private boolean isWatchOrNeutral(SignalDecision decision) {
 397:         return decision == SignalDecision.WATCH || decision == SignalDecision.NEUTRAL;
 398:     }
 399: 
 400:     private WalletSettings settings() {
 401:         return settingsRepository.findById(1L)
 402:                 .orElseGet(() -> WalletSettings.builder()
 403:                         .id(1L)
 404:                         .executionProfile(ExecutionProfile.BALANCED.name())
 405:                         .requireNewBuyTransition(true)
 406:                         .dynamicProfitLockEnabled(true)
 407:                         .profitLockActivationPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(70))
 408:                         .profitLockInitialPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(40))
 409:                         .profitLockTrailStepPercent(BigDecimal.valueOf(10))
 410:                         .build());
 411:     }
 412: 
 413:     private ValidationResult cap(ValidationResult result, HigherTimeframeBuyAssessmentService.Assessment assessment) {
 414:         if (!result.allowed() || assessment.maxInitialPercent()==null) return result;
 415:         int capped=Math.min(result.positionPercent(),assessment.maxInitialPercent());
 416:         if (capped != result.positionPercent() && fix154Metrics != null) fix154Metrics.softCappedEntry();
 417:         return capped==result.positionPercent()?result:ValidationResult.allow(capped,result.code(),result.explanation()+" FIX-154 soft-HTF cap="+capped+"%.");
 418:     }

 422:         if (referenceSignal == null || referenceSignal.getGeneratedAt() == null) return;
 423:         TradeSignal five = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, CONFIRMATION_INTERVAL);
 424:         TradeSignal one = latestAtOrBefore(referenceSignal, TREND_INTERVAL);

  33:         return sharedCandleSource == null ? legacy : sharedCandleSource.reader();

  37:     private final JdbcTemplate jdbcTemplate;
  38:     private final RegressionTestWorker worker;
  39: 
  40:     /**
  41:      * FIX-065: save multiple historical incidents/winning controls as a durable Investigation Queue.
  42:      * This is metadata only; no production trading table is read or written by this operation.
  43:      */
  44:     @Transactional
  45:     public synchronized List<Map<String, Object>> addInvestigationCases(List<RegressionInvestigationCaseRequest> requests) {
  46:         if (requests == null || requests.isEmpty()) {
  47:             throw new IllegalArgumentException("At least one investigation case is required.");
  48:         }
  49:         for (RegressionInvestigationCaseRequest request : requests) {
  50:             validateInvestigationCase(request);
  51:             String symbol = request.symbol().trim().toUpperCase(Locale.ROOT);
  52:             String caseName = request.caseName() == null || request.caseName().isBlank()
  53:                     ? symbol + " investigation" : request.caseName().trim();
  54:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
  55:                     INSERT INTO regression_investigation_case
  56:                         (case_name, symbol, start_time, end_time, wallet_id, expected_action, notes)
  57:                     VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
  58:                     """, caseName, symbol, Timestamp.from(request.startTime()), Timestamp.from(request.endTime()),
  59:                     request.walletId(), blankToNull(request.expectedAction()), blankToNull(request.notes()));
  60:         }
  61:         return investigationCases();
  62:     }
  63: 
  64:     @Transactional(readOnly = true)
  65:     public List<Map<String, Object>> investigationCases() {
  66:         return jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
  67:                 SELECT c.id, c.case_name, c.symbol, c.start_time, c.end_time, c.wallet_id,
  68:                        c.expected_action, c.notes, c.last_run_id,
  69:                        r.status AS last_run_status, r.progress_percent AS last_run_progress,
  70:                        r.completed_at AS last_run_completed_at, c.created_at, c.updated_at
  71:                 FROM regression_investigation_case c
  72:                 LEFT JOIN analysis_test_run r ON r.id = c.last_run_id
  73:                 ORDER BY c.id ASC
  74:                 """);
  75:     }
  76: 
  77:     /**
  78:      * FIX-065: running a saved case delegates to the exact same start() method as the manual form.
  79:      * Therefore the existing single-active-run backend lock and isolated replay tables stay authoritative.
  80:      */
  81:     public synchronized long startInvestigationCase(long caseId) {
  82:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList("""
  83:                 SELECT id, case_name, symbol, start_time, end_time
  84:                 FROM regression_investigation_case WHERE id = ?
  85:                 """, caseId);
  86:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Investigation case #" + caseId + " not found.");
  87:         Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0);
  88:         long runId = start(new RegressionTestRunRequest(
  89:                 String.valueOf(row.get("case_name")), String.valueOf(row.get("symbol")),
  90:                 ((Timestamp) row.get("start_time")).toInstant(), ((Timestamp) row.get("end_time")).toInstant()), ReplayDataSource.DATABASE);
  91:         jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE regression_investigation_case SET last_run_id=? WHERE id=?", runId, caseId);

 102:         if (request == null || request.symbol() == null || request.symbol().isBlank()
 103:                 || request.startTime() == null || request.endTime() == null
 104:                 || !request.endTime().isAfter(request.startTime())) {

 109:     private String blankToNull(String value) {
 110:         return value == null || value.isBlank() ? null : value.trim();
 111:     }
 112: 
 113:     /**
 114:      * FIX-089: only rows that pre-date this service/JVM instance can be leftovers from a real
 115:      * application restart. FIX-088 updated every PENDING/RUNNING row when ApplicationReadyEvent
 116:      * fired, which could falsely mark a replay created by the current JVM as interrupted.
 117:      *
 118:      * Capturing the service construction time makes the distinction deterministic: a replay row
 119:      * created after this instant belongs to the current application instance and must never be
 120:      * touched by startup recovery, even if ApplicationReadyEvent is delivered later than expected.
 121:      */
 122:     private final Instant serviceInstanceStartedAt = Instant.now();
 123: 
 124:     @EventListener(ApplicationReadyEvent.class)
 125:     public void markInterruptedRunsAfterRestart() {
 126:         jdbcTemplate.update("""
 127:                 UPDATE analysis_test_run
 128:                 SET status='ERROR', current_step='Interrupted by application restart',
 129:                     error_message='Replay was interrupted by an actual application restart. Resume is disabled; delete test data and start a new run.',
 130:                     completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 131:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
 132:                   AND created_at < ?
 133:                 """, Timestamp.from(serviceInstanceStartedAt));
 134:     }
 135: 
 136:     // FIX-088: manual Resume was removed. We intentionally do not expose a method that
 137:     // mutates an existing failed run back to PENDING. Users delete test data and start a clean run.
 138: 
 139: 
 140:     /**
 141:      * FIX-090: request cooperative cancellation of exactly one active replay worker.
 142:      * We do not mark the row ERROR here because that would be a false claim while Java is
 143:      * still executing. RegressionTestWorker marks ERROR only after it actually unwinds.
 144:      */
 145:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> stopRun(long runId) {
 146:         List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
 147:                 "SELECT id,status,current_step FROM analysis_test_run WHERE id=?", runId);
 148:         if (rows.isEmpty()) throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.NOT_FOUND, "Regression test #" + runId + " not found.");
 149:         String status = String.valueOf(rows.get(0).get("status"));
 150:         if (!("PENDING".equals(status) || "RUNNING".equals(status))) {
 151:             return Map.of("id", runId, "status", status, "activeWorker", worker.isActive(runId),
 152:                     "message", "Test is already stopped.");
 153:         }
 154:         boolean requested = worker.requestStop(runId);
 155:         if (!requested) {
 156:             // No worker in this JVM means it is already orphaned/stopped. Mark it ERROR now so
 157:             // Delete Data is not blocked forever by a stale RUNNING database status.
 158:             jdbcTemplate.update("""
 159:                     UPDATE analysis_test_run
 160:                     SET status='ERROR', current_step='Stopped by user',
 161:                         error_message='Replay worker was not active in this application instance; run marked stopped.',
 162:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6), heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)
 163:                     WHERE id=? AND status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')
 164:                     """, runId);
 165:             return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "ERROR", "activeWorker", false,
 166:                     "message", "Test had no active worker and is now stopped. You can Delete Data.");
 167:         }
 168:         jdbcTemplate.update("UPDATE analysis_test_run SET current_step='Stop requested ƒ?" waiting for replay worker to exit', heartbeat_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6) WHERE id=?", runId);
 169:         return Map.of("id", runId, "status", "STOPPING", "activeWorker", true,
 170:                 "message", "Stop requested. Delete Data will unlock after the replay worker has fully stopped.");

 248:         if (active != null && active > 0) {

 250:         }

 254:         if (!existing.isEmpty()) return Map.of("archiveBatchId", existing.get(0).get("id"), "sourceTestRunId", runId, "alreadyArchived", true);

 312:     @Transactional
 313:     public synchronized Map<String, Object> resetAllTestData() {
 314:         // FIX-090: Java worker ownership is authoritative. Database ERROR may be written a few
 315:         // milliseconds before runAsync finally releases ownership; never purge during that gap.
 316:         if (worker.hasActiveRuns()) {
 317:             throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 318:                     "A Replay/Test worker is still active. Stop it and wait for the worker to fully exit before Delete Data.");
 319:         }
 320:         Integer activeRuns = jdbcTemplate.queryForObject("""
 321:                 SELECT COUNT(*) FROM analysis_test_run
 322:                 WHERE status IN ('PENDING', 'RUNNING')
 323:                 """, Integer.class);
 324:         if (activeRuns != null && activeRuns > 0) {
 325:             List<Map<String,Object>> liveRows = jdbcTemplate.queryForList(
 326:                     "SELECT id,status FROM analysis_test_run WHERE status IN ('PENDING','RUNNING')");
 327:             boolean workerStillActive = liveRows.stream()
 328:                     .anyMatch(r -> worker.isActive(((Number) r.get("id")).longValue()));
 329:             if (workerStillActive) {
 330:                 throw new ResponseStatusException(HttpStatus.CONFLICT,
 331:                         "A Replay/Test worker is still active. Use Stop Test and wait until it becomes ERROR before Delete Data.");
 332:             }
 333:             // Defensive cleanup for a stale DB status with no Java worker.
 334:             jdbcTemplate.update("""

 336:                         error_message='No active Replay/Test worker exists in this application instance.',
 337:                         completed_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP(6)

   4: import com.crypto.wallet.repository.WalletTradeRepository;
   5: import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
   6: import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
   7: 
   8: /**
   9:  * FIX-112A: consumption belongs to the specific bullish signal, not to the
  10:  * symbol's general position state. A pre-existing position can coexist with a
  11:  * blocked progressive-add signal, so OPEN-position state is not proof that the
  12:  * immediately previous bullish signal actually executed.
  13:  */
  14: @Service
  15: @RequiredArgsConstructor
  16: public class EntryConsumptionPolicy {
  17:     private final WalletTradeRepository walletTradeRepository;
  18:     private final ExecutionReplayScope replayScope;
  19: 
  20:     public EntryConsumptionState resolve(Long previousSignalId) {
  21:         if (previousSignalId == null) return EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  22:         if (replayScope.active()) {
  23:             return replayScope.entryConsumed(previousSignalId)
  24:                     ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED
  25:                     : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  26:         }
  27:         // Production authority is the immutable executed BUY row keyed to the
  28:         // exact triggering signal. This works for both first entries and adds.
  29:         boolean executed = walletTradeRepository
  30:                 .findTopBySignalIdAndSideAndStatusOrderByExecutedAtDesc(
  31:                         previousSignalId, "BUY", "EXECUTED")
  32:                 .isPresent();
  33:         return executed ? EntryConsumptionState.CONSUMED : EntryConsumptionState.NOT_CONSUMED;
  34:     }
  35: }
